How answers are graded:
How answers are graded

- Each attempt is compared with your answer and marked right or wrong. The grade is on the whole attempt; the final answer shown is picked out automatically as a guide.
- Numbers match when they are the same value written differently: 0.675, 67.5% and 67.5 percentage points all match, and so do different scales (thousand, million, billion), thousands separators, currency symbols and numbers written as words.
- If the question asks for a particular format (a percentage, a number of decimal places, a unit, a rounding or an order), the attempt must meet it. If it asks for none, any equivalent form is accepted.
- For an ordered list, a title, honorific or article added to an entry is accepted if the order is right either with it or without it.
- Only the value the attempt finally commits to counts. Values reached while working, or considered and set aside, do not. An attempt that offers several values without choosing one is wrong, even if one of them is right.

Full answer

Total: £1,054.25 (outside London, VAT-exclusive)

Breakdown of the four items once the new fees are in force (transition rules apply the rate in force at the date of the certificate/determination):

| Work | Applicable fee/rule | Calculation | Amount | |------|---------------------|-------------|--------| | Housing legal help (opened 2024) – 10 hrs prep | Old fixed fee £157; old hourly rate £45.95. 10 hrs = £459.50, which is below the 3× escape threshold (£471), so the fixed fee is paid. | £157.00 | £157.00 | | New non‑asylum immigration legal help – 10 hrs prep | New fixed fee £317; new hourly rate £65.35. Escape threshold = 2 × £317 = £634. 10 hrs × £65.35 = £653.50, which escapes, so paid at hourly rates. | £653.50 | £653.50 | | New housing case – 3 hrs attending County Court with counsel | Housing licensed work (Table 10(ab)) – attendance harmonised at 75% of the prep rate. Lower‑court prep rate (non‑London) = £65.35, but the harmonised attendance fee is £52.00/hr. | 3 × £52.00 | £156.00 | | New discrimination case – 3 hrs attending County Court with counsel | Discrimination is not in the uplifted categories, so the old Table 10(a) rate applies: £29.25/hr. | 3 × £29.25 | £87.75 |

Sum: £157.00 + £653.50 + £156.00 + £87.75 = £1,054.25

Even counting the housing work as 13 hours (10 prep + 3 hours court attendance with counsel) and the immigration work as 10 hours, the profit-cost totals at the prescribed hourly rates stay below those thresholds. Total legal aid fee: £473 (£239 + £234).

Full answer
-----------

Outside London (non-London) the final rates are:

- Preparation/attendance/advocacy (Controlled and Licensed Work): £65.35/hour
- Travel/waiting: 50% of that = £32.70/hour
- Attendance at court/conference with counsel: 75% of the prep rate = £49.00/hour (rounded to nearest 5p)
- Routine letters/calls: harmonised to £5.20

Fixed fees and escape thresholds (outside London applies nationally for the fee, but the hourly underpin is £65.35):

- Housing legal help (Table 1): fixed fee = £223; escape threshold = 3 × £223 = £669
- Immigration non-asylum legal help – Stage 1 (Table 4(a)): fixed fee = £317; escape threshold = 2 × £317 = £634

Working out the three items:

1. Housing legal help – 10 hours preparation

Cost at hourly rates: 10 × £65.35 = £653.50 This is below the £669 escape threshold, so it stays within the fixed fee. Fee paid = £223

1. Immigration (non-asylum) legal help – 10 hours preparation

Cost at hourly rates: 10 × £65.35 = £653.50 This exceeds the £634 escape threshold, so it escapes to hourly rates. Fee paid = £653.50

1. Housing case – 3 hours attending county court with counsel

This is licensed-work attendance at court/conference with counsel (lower court), paid at 75% of the prep rate: £65.35 × 75% = £49.0125 → £49.00/hour 3 × £49.00 = £147.00

Total

£223 + £653.50 + £147.00 = £1,023.50

Full answer
-----------

Using the final rates confirmed in the consultation response (CP 1333, July 2025) and the updated tables in Annex A (and the corresponding Impact Assessment):

Outside London (non‑London) hourly floor: £65.35/hour London rate: £69.30/hour (not used here)

1. Housing legal help – 10 hours’ preparation

- Fixed fee (Table 1): £223
- Escape threshold = 3 × £223 = £669
- 10 hours × £65.35 = £653.50, which is below £669, so it does not escape.

Payable: £223 (fixed fee)

2. Non‑asylum immigration legal help – 10 hours’ preparation

- Fixed fee (Table 4(a), Stage 1 non‑asylum): £317
- Escape threshold = 2 × £317 = £634
- 10 hours × £65.35 = £653.50, which exceeds £634, so it is paid at hourly rates.

Payable: £653.50 (10 × £65.35)

3. Housing licensed work – 3 hours attending county court with counsel

- This falls under the harmonised licensed‑work attendance rate (set at 75 % of the preparation rate in the immigration/housing tables): £52.00/hour (applies nationally, rounded to nearest 5p).

Payable: £156.00 (3 × £52.00)

---

Total

| Item | Method | Amount | |------|--------|--------| | Housing legal help (10 hrs prep) | Fixed fee | £223.00 | | Immigration legal help (10 hrs prep) | Hourly (escaped) | £653.50 | | Housing court attendance with counsel (3 hrs) | Hourly | £156.00 | | Total | | £1,032.50 |

So the fees pay £1,032.50 in total (£223 fixed fee + £653.50 hourly + £156.00 hourly).

Full answer
-----------

Using the final post-consultation rates (Document 3, Annex A; Document 4):

Outside London (non-London) hourly prep/attendance rate: £65.35 Fixed fees (rounded): Housing legal help £223; Immigration (non-asylum) Stage 1 legal help £317 Escape thresholds: Housing = 3 × £223 = £669; Immigration = 2 × £317 = £634 Attendance with counsel (licensed work, county court): 75% of £65.35 = £49.0125 → £49.00/hr (rounded to nearest 5p)

Calculations

1. Housing legal help – 10 hours preparation

- 10 hrs × £65.35 = £653.50
- £653.50 < £669 escape threshold → paid the fixed fee of £223

1. Immigration (non-asylum) legal help – 10 hours preparation

- 10 hrs × £65.35 = £653.50
- £653.50 > £634 escape threshold → escapes fixed fee and is paid at hourly rates: £653.50

1. Housing case – 3 hours attending county court with counsel

- 3 hrs × £49.00 = £147.00

Total £223 + £653.50 + £147.00 = £1,023.50

Full answer
-----------

Total Final Legal Aid Fees (Outside London)

I need to calculate three components using the final fee tables from the consultation response (Document 3, Annex A), applied outside London.

1. Housing legal help — 10 hours' preparation

Housing legal help is paid as a fixed fee (Table 1):

- New fixed fee: £223 (uplifted 42% from £157)
- Escape threshold: 3 × £223 = £669
- Claim at hourly rates: 10 hrs × £65.35 = £653.50

Since £653.50 < £669, the case does not escape → provider receives the fixed fee of £223.

2. Non-asylum immigration legal help — 10 hours' preparation

Non-asylum immigration Stage 1 legal help is also a fixed fee (Table 4(a)):

- New fixed fee: £317 (uplifted 35% from £234)
- Escape threshold: 2 × £317 = £634 (legal help escape = 2× fixed fee per 2023 changes)
- Claim at hourly rates: 10 hrs × £65.35 = £653.50

Since £653.50 > £634, the case escapes → provider is paid at hourly rates: £653.50.

Calculating Total Final Legal Aid Fees (Non-London)

I need to identify the final (post-uplift) fees for three components and sum them.

1. Housing legal help — 10 hours' preparation

Legal help is Controlled Work paid at a fixed standard fee (unless escaped). From the housing controlled work fixed fees table (Annex A, Document 2):

> Housing – standard fee: £157 → £223 (42% uplift)

The fixed fee covers the entire legal help matter regardless of hours. (At £65.35/hr, 10 hours = £653.50, which is ~2.93× the standard fee — below the escape threshold, so the fixed fee applies.)

Payment: £223

2. Non-asylum immigration legal help — 10 hours' preparation

Also Controlled Work paid at a fixed standard fee. From the immigration controlled work fixed fees table:

> Immigration – non-asylum – stage 1 (legal help): £234 → £317 (35% uplift)

Payment: £317

3. Attending county court with counsel in a housing case — 3 hours

This is Licensed Work (representation at court), paid at hourly rates. The relevant fee is "Lower courts – attendance at court or conference with counsel," which is set at 75% of the corresponding preparation and attendance rate.

Non-London lower courts prep rate = £65.35 75% × £65.35 = £49.0125 → rounded to nearest 5p = £49.00/hour

3 hours × £49.00 = £147.00

Total

Calculating the Total Fees

I need the final (post-uplift) non-London rates for three pieces of work. Using the updated rate tables in Annex A of the consultation response (Document 2):

1. Housing legal help – 10 hours' preparation (non-London)

Housing & Debt Controlled Work (legal help) non-London preparation, attendance and advocacy rate (table 7(ba)):

| | | |---|---| | New rate | £65.35/hour | | 10 hours | £653.50 |

2. Non-asylum immigration legal help – 10 hours' preparation (non-London)

Immigration & Asylum Controlled Work (legal help) non-London preparation, attendance and advocacy rate (table 7(aa)):

| | | |---|---| | New rate | £65.35/hour | | 10 hours | £653.50 |

3. Housing case – 3 hours attending county court with counsel (non-London)

This is a Licensed Work fee (table 10(ab) / 10(bb)), "lower courts – attendance at court or conference with counsel." Following harmonisation, this rate is set at 75% of the non-London preparation and attendance rate:

- 75% × £65.35 = £49.0125 → rounded to nearest 5p = £49.00/hour
- 3 hours × £49.00 = £147.00

Total

| Item | Amount | |---|---| | Housing legal help, 10 hrs prep | £653.50 | | Immigration (non-asylum) legal help, 10 hrs prep | £653.50 | | Housing attendance at county court w/ counsel, 3 hrs | £147.00 | | Grand total | £1,454.00 |

Final Legal Aid Fees – Non-London Calculation

1. Housing legal help (10 hours' preparation) – Fixed fee

Housing legal help is paid as a fixed fee. Under the new rates:

| | | |---|---| | New fixed fee | £223 | | Underlying hourly rate (non-London) | £65.35 | | Value of 10 hours at hourly rates | £653.50 | | Escape threshold (3 × £223) | £669 |

Since £653.50 < £669, the claim does not escape, so the fee is the fixed fee: £223.

2. Non-asylum immigration legal help (10 hours' preparation) – Escapes to hourly rate

Non-asylum immigration legal help is also a fixed fee, but with a 2× escape threshold:

| | | |---|---| | New fixed fee | £317 | | Underlying hourly rate (non-London) | £65.35 | | Value of 10 hours at hourly rates | £653.50 | | Escape threshold (2 × £317) | £634 |

Since £653.50 > £634, the claim escapes to hourly rates: £653.50.

3. Housing – 3 hours attending county court with counsel – Hourly rate

This is Licensed Work. Under the final policy (revised from 50% to 75% following consultation), the "attendance at court or conference with counsel" rate is set at 75% of the preparation rate for the relevant court level:

- Lower Courts preparation rate (non-London): £65.35
- Attendance rate: 0.75 × £65.35 = £49.0125 → £49.00 (rounded to nearest 5p)

3 hours × £49.00 = £147.00

Total

| Item | Fee | |---|---| | Housing legal help (10 hrs) | £223 | | Non-asylum immigration legal help (10 hrs) | £653.50 | | Housing attendance at county court w/ counsel (3 hrs) | £147.00 | | Total | £1,023.50 |
